# Linux

Every project across all 39 lists that runs on Linux — **9,195** of 10,112 distinct repos, deduplicated to one row per repo.

Confirmed first, then inferred, each block by stars. Nearly every container and CI path implies Linux, so this is the widest of the four — filter on Conf. to tighten it.

[← back to the mega list](../README.md) · [6,666 inferred →](linux-inferred.md)

Page **2** of 4, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](linux.md) · **2** · [3](linux-3.md) · [4](linux-4.md) — [continue on page 3 →](linux-3.md)

Snapshot 2026-10-13. Stars, language, licence and last-push come from the GitHub API and drift daily.

The same data with screenshots embedded, filterable, is in the workbooks: [dark](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/latest/download/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-DARK.xlsx) · [light](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/latest/download/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-LIGHT.xlsx). Or filter it in the browser on the [Atlas site](https://aaa.jeremyfhall.com/catalog/).
